	else if (id === 'is.workflow.actions.ask' &amp;&amp; p.WFInputType === 'Number') p.WFAskActionDefaultAnswerNumber = decimalToE(p.WFAskActionDefaultAnswerNumber);
});

function decimalToE(value) {
	let numStr = typeof value === 'string' ? value.replace(/[\u0660-\u0669\u06f0-\u06f9]/g, d =&gt; d.charCodeAt(0) &amp; 0xf) : '';
	let m = numStr.match(/^(-?\d+)[.,٫](\d+)$/);
	return m ? parseInt(m[1] + m[2]) + 'E-' + m[2].length : value;
}</string>

const condDict = {
"":"",
"0":"&amp;lt;",
"1":"&amp;lt;=",
"2":"&amp;gt;",
"3":"&amp;gt;=",
"4":"is",
"5":"is not",
"8":"begins with",
"9":"ends with",
"99":"contains",
"100":"has any value",
"101":"does not have any value",
"999":"does not contain",
"1000":"is in the next",
"1001":"is in the last",
"1002":"is today",
"1003":"is between"};

		}

	}).replace(/\$\ufffc[78]/g, m =&gt; {
		let keys = adict.param[m].split('.');

		if (Array.isArray(kk)) {

			return kk.map(k =&gt; `&lt;span class="box"&gt;${keys.reduce((a, b) =&gt; a?.[b], k)}&lt;/span&gt;`).join(' ');

			let k = keys.reduce((a, b) =&gt; a?.[b], param);
			//delete param[keys[0]];

		}
	});
	for (let m of (name.match(/\$\ufffc\d/g) || [])) {
		name = name.replace(m, adict.param[m] || '&lt;span class="box"&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&amp;nbsp;');
	}
	if (p.length || loop &amp;&amp; flow != 2) {
		out.push(`\n&lt;button class="button ${id ? (adict.group || '') : (name.startsWith('is.workflow.actions.') || name.startsWith('com.apple.') ? 'gApple' : 'gUnknown')}${loop?' nonbottom'+(flow&gt;0?' nontop':''):''}${indent?' inside':''}${isbig?' closed':''}" id="m${i}"&gt;\n${i} ${name.replace(/^(?:com\.apple\.|is\.workflow\.actions\.)/,'')}&lt;/button&gt;&lt;div class="content${loop?' contentloop'+(['',' dotted',' dashed'][indent%3]):''}${indent?' inside':''}${a.WFWorkflowActionIdentifier==='is.workflow.actions.comment'?' commentcontent':''}"${isbig?' style="display:none"':''}&gt;`);
		out = out.concat(p);
		if (!loop || flow == 2) {
			out.push('&lt;/div&gt;');
		} else {
			indent++;
		}
	} else {
		out.push(`\n&lt;button class="${id ? (adict.group || '') : (name.startsWith('is.workflow.actions.') || name.startsWith('com.apple.') ? 'gApple' : 'gUnknown')}${loop&amp;&amp;flow&gt;0?' nontop':''}${indent?' inside':''}" id="m${i}"&gt;\n${i} ${name.replace(/^(?:com\.apple\.|is\.workflow\.actions\.)/,'')}&lt;/button&gt;`);
	}
	if (loop &amp;&amp; flow) {
		if (flow == 1) { loopStack[0].index = i; } else { loopStack.shift(); }
	}
	return out.join('').replace(new RegExp(`"magic" onclick="magictap\\(${i-1}\\)"`, 'g'), '"var"');
}).map((t,i) =&gt; {
	if (connected) {
		t = t.replace('&lt;button', '&lt;hr&gt;&lt;button');
		connected = false;
	}
	if (gVarDict[i]) {
		let d = [...new Set(gVarDict[i])];
		if (d.length == 1 &amp;&amp; d[0] == i+1) {
			connected = true;
			return t.replace('&lt;/button&gt;', `\u3000»&lt;/button&gt;`);
		} else {
			let l = d.map(a =&gt; (a == i+1) ? `&lt;i&gt;${(connected = true) ? a : a}&lt;/i&gt;` : `&lt;span class="magic" onclick="magictap(${a})"&gt;${a}&lt;/span&gt;`).join(' ');
			return t.replace('&lt;/button&gt;', `\u3000» ${l}&lt;/button&gt;`);
		}
	}
	return t;
}).join('').replace(/(&lt;button class=")button (gGray nonbottom(?: nontop)?)(?: closed)?((?:(?!&lt;button).)+?)&lt;div class="content contentloop[^&gt;]+&gt;&lt;\/div&gt;/gs,'$1$2$3').replace(/&lt;\/b&gt;&lt;b&gt;/g,'').replace(/\n&lt;\/(div|span)&gt;/g, '\n\u200b&lt;/$1&gt;').replace(/&lt;\/button&gt;\s*&lt;button/g, '&lt;\/button&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;button');

delete d.WFWorkflowActions;
let paramstr = JSON.stringify(Object.keys(d).sort().reduce((o,k)=&gt;(o[k]=d[k],o),{}),null,3).replace(/^\{\n|^\}$|^   /gm, '').replace(/WFWorkflowMinimumClientVersion": (\d+),?/, (a,b) =&gt; a + ` &lt;span style="font-style:italic;${parseInt(b)&gt;5000?'color:red':''}"&gt;(iOS ${{467: 12, 900:13, 1106:15, 1113:15, 2025:17, 3010:18, 3216:18.2, 5001:27, 5023:27}[b] || '??'})&lt;/span&gt;`);
paramstr = paramstr.replace(/"ActionIndex": (\d+)/g, '"ActionIndex": &lt;span class="magic" onclick="magictap($1)"&gt;&lt;b&gt;$1&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/span&gt;');
paramstr = paramstr.replace(/"__value__": (\d{10})\b,?/g, (m,p) =&gt; m+` (&lt;span style="font-style:italic"&gt;${new Date(p+'000'-(new Date()).getTimezoneOffset()*60000).toISOString().slice(0, -5).replace('T',' ')})&lt;/span&gt;`);

let shortcuttitle = (shortcutname.length &gt; shortcutname.normalize().length) ? shortcutname.split('').join('\u2009') : shortcutname;

else if (loopStack.length) shortcuttitle = `&lt;span class="magic" onclick="magictap(${loopStack[0].index})" style="color:red"&gt;‼️&lt;/span&gt; ` + shortcuttitle;

&lt;div class="col2"&gt;${paramstr}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`;


function ifCondition(param) {
	let cond = param.WFCondition?.toString() || '';
	let out = `&lt;span class="box"&gt;${condDict[cond]}&lt;/span&gt;`;
	if (cond !== '') {
		if (cond !== '100' &amp;&amp; cond !== '101' &amp;&amp; cond !== '1002') {
			let paramk = Object.keys(param);
			let m = [];
			if (cond === '1003') {
				m = [['WFNumberValue','WFAnotherNumber'],['WFDate','WFAnotherDate'],['WFDuration','WFAnotherDuration'],['WFMeasurement','WFAnotherMeasurement']].filter(c =&gt; paramk.includes(c[0]) &amp;&amp; paramk.includes(c[1]));
				if (m.length == 1) {
					out += ` &lt;span class="box"&gt;${gettext(param[m[0][0]])}&lt;/span&gt; and &lt;span class="box"&gt;${gettext(param[m[0][1]])}&lt;/span&gt;`;
					delete param[m[0][0]];
					delete param[m[0][1]];
				}
			} else {
				let b;
				isbool = cond === '4' &amp;&amp;
					(b = param.WFInput?.Variable?.Value?.Aggrandizements) &amp;&amp;
					(b[0].CoercionItemClass === 'WFBooleanContentItem' ||
					b.some(a =&gt; ['Is Me','Is a Screenshot','Is Hidden','Is Favorite','Is Explicit','Is Cloud Item','Has Album Artwork','Is Streamable','Is Universal','Supports Game Center','Has Photo','enabled'].includes(a.PropertyName)));
				if (isbool) {
					out = 'is true';
				} else {
					m = ['WFConditionalActionString','WFNumberValue','WFMeasurement','WFDuration','WFDate','WFEnumeration','WFLinkEnumeration'].filter(c =&gt; paramk.includes(c));
					if (m.length == 1) {
						out += ` &lt;span class="box"&gt;${isNaN(parseInt(param.WFLinkEnumeration)) ? gettext(param[m[0]]) : ['Portrait','Portrait Upside Down','Landscape Left','Landscape Right','Face Up','Face Down','Unknown'][parseInt(param.WFLinkEnumeration)]}&lt;/span&gt;`;
						delete param[m[0]];
					}
				}
			}
			if (m.length &gt; 1) {
				out += ' &lt;span class="box" style="display:inline-grid; line-height:normal;"&gt;';
				m.forEach(a =&gt; {
					out += `&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;div class="col1"&gt;${a.slice(2)}&lt;/div&gt;\n&lt;div class="col2"&gt;${gettext(param[a])}&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;`;
					delete param[a];
				});
				out += '&lt;/span&gt;';
			}
		}
	}
	return `&lt;span class="box"&gt;${gettext(param.WFInput || '')}&lt;/span&gt; ` + out;
}

function gettext(i) {
	switch (typeof(i)) {
		case 'object':
			if (Array.isArray(i)) {
				return ('[' + i.map(a =&gt; gettext(a)).join(',\n') + ']').replace(/&lt;span class=\\"[^&gt;]+&gt;/g, m =&gt; m.replace(/\\"/g,'"'));
			} else {
				if (i.Type) {
					if (i.Type === 'Variable') {
						i = i.Variable;
					} else {
						return '?type?'+i.Type;
					}
				}
				if (Number.isInteger(i.WFItemType)) {
					return stringifyMap(getvalue(i)).replace(/(  +|^)\{\n +/g,'$1{  ').replace(/( *)(\{.*)\n\1\}/g,'$1$2 }');
				}
				let t = i.WFSerializationType || '';
				switch (t) {
					case 'WFTextTokenAttachment':
						return variablename(i.Value);
					case 'WFTextTokenString':
						return stringify(i.Value);
					case 'WFDictionaryFieldValue':
						return stringifyMap(getdict(i.Value)).replace(/&lt;span class=\\"[^&gt;]+&gt;/g, m =&gt; m.replace(/\\"/g,'"')).replace(/(  +|^)\{\n +/g,'$1{  ').replace(/( *)(\{.*)\n\1\}/g,'$1$2 }');
					case 'WFQuantityFieldValue':
						return variablename(i.Value.Magnitude || '') + ' ' + i.Value.Unit;
					case 'WFINSpeakableStringSubstitutableState':
						return i.Value ? escapeStr(i.Value.DisplayString) : '';
					case 'WFNumberStringSubstitutableState':
					case 'WFDateSubstitutableState':
						return gettext(i.Value);
					case 'WFContactFieldValue':
						return '[' + (i.Value.WFContactFieldValues || []).map(a =&gt; JSON.stringify(a).replace(/\{"__type__":"data","__value__":"([^"]*)"/g, (m,p1) =&gt; '"'+(new TextDecoder().decode(base64DecToArr(p1))).replace(/\n/g,'\n   ')+'"')).join(',\n') + ']';
					case 'WFContentPredicateTableTemplate':
						if (i.Value.WFActionParameterFilterTemplates?.[0]?.Property) return predicate(i.Value);
					default:
						if (i.string) { return stringify(i); }
						if (i['__type__'] === 'date') {
							return new Date(1000*i['__value__']).toLocaleString();
						}
						return escapeStr(JSON.stringify(i,null,3)).replace(/\\n/g,'\n').replace(/\\t/g,'\t').replace(/(  +|^)\{\n +/g,'$1{  ').replace(/( *)(\{.*)\n\1\}/g,'$1$2 }');
				}
			}
		case 'string': return escapeStr(i);
		default: return i;
	}
}

function b64ToUint6(nChr) {
	return nChr &gt; 64 &amp;&amp; nChr &lt; 91 ?
		  nChr - 65
		: nChr &gt; 96 &amp;&amp; nChr &lt; 123 ?
		  nChr - 71
		: nChr &gt; 47 &amp;&amp; nChr &lt; 58 ?
		  nChr + 4
		: nChr === 43 ?
		  62
		: nChr === 47 ? 63 : 0;
}

function base64DecToArr(sBase64) {
	let
		sB64Enc = sBase64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9\+\/]/g, ""), nInLen = sB64Enc.length,
		nOutLen = nInLen * 3 + 1 &gt;&gt; 2,
		taBytes = new Uint8Array(nOutLen);

	for (let nMod3, nMod4, nUint24 = 0, nOutIdx = 0, nInIdx = 0; nInIdx &lt; nInLen; nInIdx++) {
		nMod4 = nInIdx &amp; 3;
		nUint24 |= b64ToUint6(sB64Enc.charCodeAt(nInIdx)) &lt;&lt; 6 * (3 - nMod4);
		if (nMod4 === 3 || nInLen - nInIdx === 1) {
			for (nMod3 = 0; nMod3 &lt; 3 &amp;&amp; nOutIdx &lt; nOutLen; nMod3++, nOutIdx++) {
				taBytes[nOutIdx] = nUint24 &gt;&gt;&gt; (16 &gt;&gt;&gt; nMod3 &amp; 24) &amp; 255;
			}
			nUint24 = 0;
		}
	}
	return taBytes;
}

function truncate(str, max, vsize) {
	let m = str.match(/&lt;span.+?&lt;\/span&gt;/g);
	let n1 = 0;
	let s = str;
	if (m) {
		n1 = m.length;
		s = s.replace(/&lt;span.+?&lt;\/span&gt;/g, '\ufffc');
	}
	if (s.length &gt; max) {
		str = s.substring(0, max - 20);
		str += vsize ? `\n&lt;b&gt;&lt;i&gt;(${vsize.toString().slice(0,-3)} kbytes; content truncated)&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/b&gt;` : ' &lt;b&gt;&lt;i&gt;(truncated)&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/b&gt;';
		if (m) {
			let n2 = (str.match(/\ufffc/g) || []).length;
			for (let i=0; i&lt;n2; i++) {
				str = str.replace('\ufffc', m[i]);
			}
			if (n1 &gt; n2) {
				str += `${vsize ? '\n' : ''}…… ${m.slice(n2).join(', ')}`;
			}
		}
	}
	return str;
}

function predicate(v) {
	let filters = v.WFActionParameterFilterTemplates;
	let out = filters.length&gt;1 ? `• ${v.WFActionParameterFilterPrefix==0 ? 'Any of' : 'All'} the following are true\n` : '';
	out += filters.map(f =&gt; {
		let p = f.Property || '';
		if (p.includes('Date') || p === 'Birthday') { return filtertext(f, {'type':'Date'}); }
		if (p.startsWith('Is ') || p.startsWith('Has ')) { return filtertext(f, {'type':'Bool'}); }
		let d = {'type':'String'};
		if (p !== 'Album' || !f.Values.String) { return filtertext(f, filterDict[p] || d); }
		return filtertext(f, d);
	}).join('\n');
	return out;
}

function filtertext(f, d) {
	function mapNullUndef(a, b) { return (a == null || a == undefined) ? b : a; }
	let p = f.Property || '?prop?';
	let v = f.Values;
	let t = d.type;
	let o = condDict[f.Operator.toString()] || '?op?';
	let out = p + ' ' + o + ' ';
	switch (t) {
		case 'Bool':
			return v.Bool ? p : p.replace(' ', ' Not ');
		case 'Enumeration':
			return out + (mapNullUndef(v.Enumeration ? gettext(v.Enumeration.Value) : v.Enumeration, mapNullUndef(d.default, '(default)')));
		case 'Date':
			if (f.Operator == 1002) { return out; }
			if (f.Operator == 1003) { return out + gettext(mapNullUndef(v.Date, '(now)')) + ' and ' + gettext(mapNullUndef(v.AnotherDate, '(now)')); }
			if (f.Operator &gt; 999) {
				t = 'Number';
				d = {'unit':'Unit'};
			} else if ([null, undefined].includes(v.Date)) { v.Date = '(now)'; }
		default:
			out += gettext(mapNullUndef(v[t], mapNullUndef(v.Number, JSON.stringify(v))));
			let u = d.unit;
			if (u) {
				let unitv = mapNullUndef(v[u]?.Value, mapNullUndef(v[u], ((p === 'Duration') ? 32 : 4)));
				return out + ' ' + unitDict[u][unitv.toString()];
			}
			return  out;
	}
}

function variablename(v) {
	if (typeof(v) !== 'object') { return escapeStr(JSON.stringify(v)); }
	let t = v.Type || '';
	switch (t) {
		case 'Variable':
			return '&lt;span class="var"&gt;&lt;b&gt;['+v.VariableName + aggr(v);
		case 'ActionOutput':
			let u = v.OutputUUID;
			if (u &amp;&amp; Number.isInteger(udict[u])) {
				let i = udict[u];
				if (gVarDict[i]) {
					gVarDict[i].push(gIndex);
				} else {
					gVarDict[i] = [gIndex];
				}
				return `&lt;span class="magic" onclick="magictap(${i})"&gt;&lt;b&gt;[${i} ` + v.OutputName + aggr(v);
			}
			return '&lt;span class="var" style="color:red"&gt;&lt;b&gt;[? ' + v.OutputName + aggr(v);
		default:
			return '&lt;span class="var"&gt;&lt;b&gt;[' + t.replace('ExtensionInput', 'Shortcut Input') + (v.Prompt ? ' “&lt;/b&gt;' + escapeStr(v.Prompt) + '&lt;b&gt;”' : '') + aggr(v);
	}
}

function stringify(o) {
	let out = escapeStr(o.string);
	if (o.attachmentsByRange) {
		Object.entries(o.attachmentsByRange)
			.sort((a,b) =&gt; parseInt(a[0].substring(1)) - parseInt(b[0].substring(1)))
			.forEach(a =&gt; {
				out = out.replace('\ufffc', variablename(a[1]));
		});
	}
	return out;
}

function aggr(o) {
	let out = '';
	for (let a of (o.Aggrandizements || [])) {
		switch (a.Type) {
			case 'WFCoercionVariableAggrandizement':
				let c = a.CoercionItemClass;
				out += ' as ' + c.slice(2, -11).replace('String', 'Text').replace('Workflow', 'Shortcut').replace(/^$/, 'Anything');
				break;
			case 'WFDictionaryValueVariableAggrandizement':
				out += `.${a.DictionaryKey}`;
				break;
			case 'WFPropertyVariableAggrandizement':
				out += '’s ' + (a.NegativePropertyName || a.PropertyName);
				break;
			default:
				delete a.Type;
				out += escapeStr(JSON.stringify(a).replace('WFDateFormatStyle','Date Style').replace('WFTimeFormatStyle','Time Style').replace('WFRelativeDateFormatStyle','Relative Style'));
		}
	}
	return (out === '') ? `]&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/span&gt;` : `&lt;/b&gt;${out}&lt;b&gt;]&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/span&gt;`;
}

function getdict(dict) {
	const out = new Map();
	for (const d of dict.WFDictionaryFieldValueItems) {
		let v = getvalue(d);
		out.set(stringify(d.WFKey.Value) + ' ' + Math.random().toString().replace('.',''), (mobile &amp;&amp; typeof(v) === 'string') ? truncate(v, 150) : v);
	}
	return out;
}

function getvalue(d) {
	if (typeof d !== 'object') { return escapeStr(d); }
	let v = d.WFValue ? d.WFValue.Value : null;
	switch (d.WFItemType) {
		case 0: return stringify(v) + (d.WFValue.Synonyms ? ' &lt;i&gt;Synonyms&lt;/i&gt;[' + d.WFValue.Synonyms.map(a=&gt;gettext(a)).join(', ') + ']' : '');
		case 1: return d.WFValue.WFSerializationType?.includes('Substitutable') ? variablename(v.Value) + ' &lt;i&gt;as Dictionary&lt;/i&gt;' : getdict(v.Value);
		case 2: return d.WFValue.WFSerializationType?.includes('Substitutable') ? variablename(v.Value) + ' &lt;i&gt;as Array&lt;/i&gt;' : v.map(getvalue);
		case 3: return stringify(v) + ' &lt;i&gt;as Number&lt;/i&gt;';
		case 4: return v.Value ? variablename(v.Value) + ' &lt;i&gt;as Boolean&lt;/i&gt;' : v;
		case 5: return variablename(v ? v.Value : {Type:'Choose'});
	}
}

function stringifyMap(o) {
	let px = '   ';
	if (typeof o === 'object') {
		if (Array.isArray(o)) return o.length ? '[\n'+ px + o.map(a=&gt;stringifyMap(a)).join(',\n').replace(/\n/g,'\n'+px) + '\n]' : '[]';
		let oo = Array.from(o.entries());
		return oo.length ? '{\n' + px + oo.map(a=&gt;stringifyMap(a[0]).replace(/ \d+"$/,'"')+': '+stringifyMap(a[1])).join(',\n').replace(/\n/g,'\n'+px) + '\n' + '}' : '{}';
	}
	let s = JSON.stringify({a:o}).slice(5, -1).replace(/&lt;b&gt;\\\\u/g, '&lt;b&gt;\\u');
	if (s.endsWith('&lt;/i&gt;"')) {
		return s.includes('&lt;span') ? s.slice(1,-1) : s.replace(/^"| &lt;i&gt;as (Number|Boolean)&lt;\/i&gt;"$/g, '');
	} else {
		return s;
	}
}

function escapeStr(s) {
	return s.replace(/&amp;/g,'&amp;amp;').replace(/&lt;/g,'&amp;lt;')
		.replace(/[\0-\x09\x20]+$/gm, c=&gt;c.replace(/\x09/g,'&lt;b&gt;\\u{tab}&lt;/b&gt;').replace(/\x20/g,'&lt;b&gt;\\u{space}&lt;/b&gt;'))
		.replace(/[\0-\x08\x0B-\x1F\x7F-\xA0\xAD\u2000-\u200B\u2028\u2029\u202F\u205F-\u2064\u2800\u3000]/g, c=&gt;'&lt;b&gt;\\u'+c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(4,'0')+'&lt;/b&gt;')
		.replace(/[\uD8C0-\uDB7F][\uDC00-\uDFFF]/g, c =&gt; '\\u' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(4,'0') + '\u200B\\u' + c.charCodeAt(1).toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(4,'0') + '\u200B');
}</string>

&lt;script&gt;
for (b of document.getElementsByClassName('button')) {
	b.addEventListener('click', function() {
		this.classList.toggle('closed');
		let s = this.nextElementSibling;
		s.style.display = getComputedStyle(s).display==='none' ? 'block' : 'none';
})}

function magictap(id) {
	event.stopPropagation();
	let e = document.getElementById('m'+id);
	let p = e;
	while ((p = p.parentNode).tagName.toLowerCase() === 'div') {
		p.style.display = 'block';
		p.previousElementSibling.classList.remove('closed');
	}
	setTimeout(function(){ e.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'}); }, 10);
}

&lt;div id="input" contenteditable autofocus spellcheck="false" autocorrect="off" autocapitalize="none" style="width: 100%; min-height: 300px; font-family: monospace; font-size: 15px; word-break: break-all; white-space: pre-wrap; tab-size: 2; caret-color: red; outline: none;"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;/body&gt;&lt;script&gt;
/*
	Syntax highlighting adapted from:
	https://codepen.io/absolutedevelopment/pen/EpwVzN
*/
let timeoutID;
let t = document.title;
let s = &#xFFFC;.source.replace(/&amp;/g, "&amp;amp;").replace(/&lt;/g, "&amp;lt;").replace(/&gt;/g, "&amp;gt;");
document.getElementById('input').innerHTML = s[0]==='{' ? JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(s),null,'\t')
.replace(/[\0-\x08\x0B-\x1F\x7F-\xA0\xAD\u2000-\u200B\u2028\u2029\u202F\u205F-\u2064\u2800\u3000\uFFFC]/g, c=&gt;'\\u'+c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(4,'0'))
.replace(/("(\\u[a-zA-Z0-9]{4}|\\[^u]|[^\\"])*"(\s*:)?|\b(true|false|null)\b|-?\d+(?:\.\d*)?(?:[eE][+\-]?\d+)?)/g, (m)=&gt;`&lt;span class="${/^"/.test(m) ? (/:$/.test(m) ? 'key' : 'string') : (/true|false/.test(m) ? 'bool' : 'number')}"&gt;${m}&lt;/span&gt;`) :
s.replace(/[\0-\x08\x0B-\x1F\x7F-\xA0\xAD\u2000-\u200B\u2028\u2029\u202F\u205F-\u2064\u2800\u3000\uFFFC]/g, c =&gt; '&amp;\u0023x'+c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).toUpperCase()+';')
.replace(/&amp;lt;(key|string|integer|real)&amp;gt;(.*?)&amp;lt;\/\1&amp;gt;/g, (m, p1, p2)=&gt;`&amp;lt;${p1}&amp;gt;&lt;span class="${p1==='key' ? 'key' : (p1==='string' ? 'string' : 'number')}"&gt;${p2}&lt;/span&gt;&amp;lt;/${p1}&amp;gt;`)
.replace(/&amp;lt;(true|false)\/&amp;gt;/g, '&lt;span class="bool"&gt;$&amp;&lt;/span&gt;');

function finish() {
  clearTimeout(timeoutID);
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(document.getElementById('input').innerText);
  document.title = '✅ Close window to continue';
  timeoutID = setTimeout(() =&gt; { document.title = t; }, 5000);
}
